**`[~]` items say when they are done (2026-09-27):** put what is left as one or
more markers on the item, written out in full and ANDed together:
``done-when: sql `SELECT <one column>` => <expected>`` (read-only, first row),
`done-when: test <path>`, `done-when: issue #N closed`, or `done-when: pr #N merged`.
Only add markers when they cover everything that is left (a screenshot or a
review still to do means no marker yet). `node scripts/open-done-when.mjs` runs them;
the nightly `open-done-when` workflow opens one nightly-red issue listing the items
that are ready to tick. src/test/openPartlyDoneItemsSayDoneWhen.test.ts ratchets the
count of `[~]` items with no marker.

   that name, so it closes the "Sentry not synced" ledger item on the next hourly run.

   minutes (7 times since 22:29Z on 2026-09-22, last 2026-09-23 04:00Z) with

4. **Should seed/test accounts receive real email? (Q2/Q29)** Every dead
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-up): keep sending to test accounts until launch; stop at launch (on the launch checklist as Q144).**
   letter so far went to a test account, and the cause was Resend's DAILY
   QUOTA: the 2026-09-13 seed-heavy run enqueued 319 emails in one day. Test
   mail spends the same quota real users' sign-in and receipt mail needs. The
   option: skip sending to is_seed recipients (log it as `skipped_seed` in
   email_send_log), with an allowlist for the journeys that assert delivery.
   Your call, because it changes what the E2E journeys can check.
5. **Push notifications (Q82): three things only you can do.** The code
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "cut it when main is current".** Claude runs fastlane beta from main
   once the open landings are in and main CI is green; you install it and open the app once.
   cause is fixed and pushed (see Q82), but no phone has the fix yet.
   (a) **Cut a TestFlight build from main** (at or after the Q82 fix commit)
   and install it on your iPhone. The only builds with the AppDelegate
   forwarding are ce4d38d59 (2026-09-02) and later, and every build so far
   also has the boot race (it depends on timing, so it may not hit every
   launch). (b) **On that build: sign in, go to Home, tap "Enable" on the
   notifications pill, then Allow.** The pill only shows after you've posted
   or applied, or on a later launch more than an hour after install. If you tapped "Don't
   Allow" on any earlier build, iOS will not ask again: turn it on in
   Settings > Helpr > Notifications first, then cold-launch the app. Within
   seconds `select count(*) from push_tokens` should be 1 and
   analytics_events should show `push_token_saved`. If it doesn't, the
   analytics events say which step failed. (c) **Only if you run the app
   straight from Xcode on your phone:** those builds get SANDBOX APNs tokens,
   and prod sends to the production APNs host (`APNS_USE_SANDBOX` is unset),
   so APNs rejects them with BadDeviceToken and the sender deletes the row.
   TestFlight/App Store builds are unaffected. Leave this alone unless you
   test from Xcode. APNs credentials are fine: APNS_KEY_ID, APNS_TEAM_ID
   (digest matches P85MCK558V), APNS_BUNDLE_ID (digest matches com.Helpr)
   and APNS_AUTH_KEY are all set in Supabase secrets. Whether the .p8 key
   itself is valid can't be checked without a real token to send to.

7. **Turn off Supabase's GitHub auto-deploy to production (Q121). Top priority.**
   On every push to main, Supabase's GitHub integration applies new migrations
   (and deploys edge functions) to prod within about a minute. Our deploy
   workflow's safety checks (lint, replay test, destructive-DDL stop) run after
   that, so for the last 24h (24 migrations) they have not blocked anything.
   Measured from prod's own logs 2026-09-23 (Q117). What you do: Supabase
   dashboard > Project Settings > Integrations > GitHub, and switch off
   automatic deploys to the production branch (exact toggle name not verified).
   Until then, each new migration turns db-deploy red by design (the new
   provenance check), so the side channel stays visible.
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner asked "whats the best option?"): switch it OFF.** Measured: db-deploy
   36261725456 (18:16Z) judged 124 prod versions since the cutoff, 108 with a db-deploy receipt and 16
   acknowledged side-channel applies, the newest of them 2026-09-23 ~16:10Z (Q194). So nothing has come
   in by the side channel for about 3 days, but that is not proof the toggle is off. Off is the only
   setting where db-deploy's lint, replay and destructive-DDL checks actually run before prod changes.
   Still the owner's dashboard click; the provenance check stays as the detector either way.

7. **Backups (Q45): the restore is proven weekly now; five things are yours.**
   **ANSWERED 2026-09-23 (owner pop-ups): (a) yes, Pro is paid and intended; (b) keep 14-day retention; (c) back up uploaded files EXCEPT id-documents, encrypted (work item Q147); (d) no PITR (no paid upgrade); (e) BACKUP_PASSPHRASE and ban_fingerprint_salt go in the owner's password manager (owner to-do).**
   Measured 2026-09-23. Nothing below is needed for the drill to stay green.
   (a) **Is the Supabase org really on Pro?** The API says `plan: "pro"`, and
   `supabase backups list` shows 7 daily platform backups. Every doc said
   "free tier". If you are paying $25/mo, you already have in-place daily
   restores for 7 days. If you did not mean to be on Pro, that is a bill.
   (b) **Backup retention is 14 days, not 90.** The repo caps artifacts and
   logs at 14 days (`maximum_allowed_days` 90). Raising it to 90 is one
   setting, but it lengthens EVERY workflow's artifacts, and on the free
   GitHub plan that can go past the artifact-storage quota (billed). The
   alternative is a monthly copy somewhere else. Your call.
   (c) **Storage files (photos, ID documents) are in NO backup**: 152 objects,
   about 10 MB (proof-photos 64, job-photos 61, message-attachments 18,
   user-documents 4, avatars 4, id-documents 1). The platform backup excludes
   them too. Backing them up needs a service-role key as an Actions secret and
   puts ID documents (encrypted) into a GitHub artifact: a privacy trade-off.
   (d) **PITR** (restore to the minute) is a paid add-on, about $100/mo plus
   Small compute. Without it, worst-case loss is about 24 h (platform) or
   24 h + GitHub's up-to-7 h schedule delay (ours). You said no paid upgrades,
   so this is only the measured gap.
   (e) **Two secrets that no backup holds.** `BACKUP_PASSPHRASE`: is there a
   copy outside GitHub? If not, losing the GitHub account also loses every
   backup. `ban_fingerprint_salt` (vault): it cannot be re-created, and
   without it every stored ban fingerprint stops matching after a restore.
   Want it (encrypted) in the backup, or kept in your password manager?

29. **PR #1810 (Q392): which parish job-match alert design stays? (2026-09-26).** PR #1810 and main
    (20260926041132, Q225/V-008) both fix "parish alert arrives before early access shows the job", differently:
    (A) KEEP MAIN'S queue-only design (every send goes through the 1-minute sweep, so elite/no-delay users hear up
    to ~60s later; nothing sent inside the funding write) and ADD the PR's block check (`user_blocks`, both ways)
    and shared `job_announceable_to()` gate. A user who deletes the alert can be told again if the job reopens.
    (B) TAKE THE PR'S design: elite/no-delay users told inline at funding (incl. email via net.http_post inside the
    escrow write), a permanent per-(user, job) `job_match_queue` ledger so nobody is ever told twice, block check;
    main's `parish_match_alert_queue` and its sweep step are retired.
    Measured live (pg_get_functiondef, prod): neither `notify_helpers_on_job_post` nor `deliver_parish_match_alert`
    checks `user_blocks` today; `deliver_job_match`/`job_announceable_to` do not exist on prod.
    Not blocked by this: the PR's instant-match half, digest gating, export_my_data line, and Q401 (auth-page
    noindex) are being landed separately. The PR's old-timestamp migrations must never reach main as-is.
    **ANSWERED 2026-09-26 (owner pop-up): "A + never twice": keep main's queue-only send path, add the
    `user_blocks` check (both ways) and `job_announceable_to()` gate, AND write parish sends into the permanent
    per-(user, job) ledger so a deleted alert is never re-sent when the job reopens. Lands after the instant half.**
    **DONE 2026-09-26: landed as migration 20260926195608 (Q392 parish half).**
30. **press-every-control: press one representative per repeated list row? (#1582, 2026-09-26).** Run
    36230166945: /admin?view=jobs alone took 97 min (412 presses), mostly the same four controls on every job
    card. Landed without asking: shards now claim rows from one shared queue (Q725), so a heavy row no longer
    strands the rest of its shard. Still yours: (A) keep pressing every card (complete, slow; ~97 min a run on
    that view), or (B) press every control on the first N cards of a repeated list and only count the rest
    (much faster; a defect that shows only on card N+1 would be missed). Default until answered: (A).
31. **Group crews: three money rules (Q728/Q729, 2026-09-27).** Group jobs are off
    (GROUP_JOBS_ENABLED=false), so nothing is charged under these yet. (A) CREW_FEE_PAYS_UNCONFIRMED:
    a late-cancel fee is shared by a member the poster OFFERED the job to but who never confirmed.
    Built as yes (your Q407(13) answer); confirm. (B) CREW_COMPLETES_WHEN_HIRED_DONE: an under-filled
    crew completes when every hired member is done, and the unfilled shares go back to the poster.
    Built as yes; confirm. (C) New: when a crew dispute or an unfilled slot returns a member's share,
    only the share itself (budget + urgent fee) goes back. The platform fee, sales tax and any gift
    card on that charge are NOT returned. Should they be returned in proportion to the share?
    ANSWERED 2026-09-27: yes, pro-rata. BUILT (see the ANSWERED block above); guard src/test/edge/process-scheduled-payouts-crew.test.ts.

## FEEDS — mirrored from the alert ledger, nightly-red issues and the audit bus (node scripts/open-sync-trackers.mjs)

Each item mirrors one open problem; its `feed:` tags are sticky (never edit them) and its `done-when:` holds once the source closes, when the sync flips it to `[~]`. The same problem seen again while its item is open is attached to that item (marked Seen again), never filed twice; every red overnight check is one sub-line of the ONE Fix the test robots item, which the sync rewrites.

- [~] **Q865 MEDIUM** (nightly-red #1754 prod-audit, 2026-09-30): 156 of run 36766443019's 158 skips are NOT justified. deep-links, messy-input and interruptions call `ensureFundedOpenJob` in `beforeAll`; under live Stripe it calls `skipLivePay` (`test.skip`), which in a `beforeAll` skips EVERY test in the file, including those that never touch the funded job (signed-out redirects, gone-job, /user/:id, the six /profile?tab= links). Only tests that read `fx.openJob` should skip; they already carry per-test `test.skip(!fx.openJob, …)`. The other 2 skips (expanding-search) are justified. Fix: let the fixture return "none" in live mode instead of skipping, keep the live-pay skip per test, and GUARD the class: a `beforeAll` must not call a fixture that can `test.skip` (static scan of e2e/prod-audit, @mutate one spec back). Then re-measure `prod-audit.perTest`: set to 138.2 (the max of runs 36744778439 = 138.2 and 36766443019 = 137.7, both with 91 tests run) so the budget step passes today. Run 36364639347 (before the switch, 124 tests) measured 109.9, which is inside the two-way band, so the un-skip will not trip it; lower it to the new measurement in the same commit. done-when: issue #2245 closed (tick when a prod-audit run shows only justified skips, and `prod-audit.perTest` equals its measured value). **Fixed 2026-09-30 (Q865 lane):** the three beforeAll hooks now mint inside `unlessLivePay(() => ...)` (e2e/prod-audit/fundedOpenJob.ts), which turns the live-pay skip into a value; only tests that open a funded fixture skip, per test, via `skipWhenUnfundedLive` -> `skipLivePay`. Of the 156 (`playwright test --list`): deep-links 18 of 21 un-skipped, interruptions 5 of 12 (the 7 apply tests still skip), messy-input 112 of 123 (4 applicant sweeps, 2 pending-applicant sweeps and 5 openJob/pending/disputed explores still skip); 135 un-skipped, 21 skip with the live-pay reason. Guard: `src/test/journeysNeverPayLive.test.ts` "no beforeAll mints a funded fixture outside unlessLivePay" (2 @mutate lines, both killed). **perTest NOT changed:** the un-skipped tests now run, so `prod-audit.perTest` must be RE-MEASURED from the next real prod-audit run and set to that value (not 138.2, which was measured with 91 tests). Same mechanism as the owner's 10-09 21:45Z incident (commit 60de96aeb): getSession() returns null when the stored access token has expired and the refresh fails, and supabase-js then sends the anon key. 60de96aeb/63dc73e2a/24bed73f1 (landed 2026-10-11, not in either failing release d9342ecf/c123c618) stop the false sign-out but NOT the anon requests. Since 2026-10-10 14:39Z: 0 events (Sentry `message:*permission denied for*`, 7d, read 04:2xZ). The iOS burst 10-09 13:38-13:40Z ran 2.5 min across /home, /post-job, /profile, /messages, /jobs, so it is not only a sub-second wake race. Fix still to build (an auth change, so it carries a Sensitive check trailer): before a /rest/v1 or /functions/v1 request leaves with the anon bearer while auth-js still holds a session that failed to refresh on the network, wait for TOKEN_REFRESHED (auth-js caches a failed refresh for REFRESH_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS = 60 s, so a retry inside that window gets the cached failure) and send with the user's token, else send as today; guard on the real supabase-js client like src/lib/authSignOut.lateLogout.test.ts. **ONE ROOT CAUSE, MERGED 2026-10-14** (owner: items that share one root cause are one item). Merged here: Q1597 (dashboard.ctx applications), Q1598 (backout_notices), Q1600 (job_schedule_change_requests), Q1601 (get_job_offer_targets), Q1602 (applications), Q1603 (jobs), Q1609 (referral_codes), Q1620 (get_my_saved_helpers), Q1630 (login_history), Q1811 (profiles again, new bundle hash), Q1621 (missing authorization header), Q1636 and Q1812 (session lost on a signed-in screen), Q1610 and Q1700 (functionshttperror, one Sentry issue JAVASCRIPT-1Q), Q1599 and Q1584 (error screen "we couldn't load this"); recurring_visit_payments, payout_transfers and gift_cards also fired in the same bursts. MEASURED 2026-10-14: (1) Sentry, 100 newest "permission denied for" events (14 d): 98 on iOS with NO Sentry user (src/main.tsx sets the user from the Supabase session, so no user = no session), on signed-in routes (/home, /posts, /jobs, /profile, /post-job, /messages), in bursts 2026-10-09 01:38Z, 02:48Z, 13:37-13:40Z, then 10-10 02:51Z/12:00Z/14:39Z and 10-13 22:57Z/23:01Z (profiles only); 2 on Mac OS with a user (10-09 21:45Z). (2) The "session lost on a signed-in screen" events (JAVASCRIPT-3H, iOS, no user) carry the SAME timestamps as the 10-10 and 10-13 profiles events; "missing authorization header" (JAVASCRIPT-35) and the real functionshttperror events (JAVASCRIPT-1Q, 10-09 02:48Z and 13:38Z, iOS, no user) sit inside the bursts (1Q's 10-12 events are automated, the test poster). The error-screen ledger rows for /posts and the Sentry "we couldn't load this" rows were first and last seen inside the same bursts. (3) Grants on prod: every table/function named is granted to `authenticated` and NOT to `anon` (applications, jobs: column SELECT for authenticated only; backout_notices, job_schedule_change_requests, referral_codes, login_history, recurring_visit_payments, payout_transfers, gift_cards: table SELECT for authenticated only; get_job_offer_targets, get_my_saved_helpers: EXECUTE for authenticated only; profiles: anon may SELECT but only authenticated may UPDATE, 87 columns). So the queries ran as anon. NOT YET KNOWN: why the iOS app lost its session while showing a signed-in screen (Q1581 /jobs error screen and Q1631 unexpected sign-out stay separate: Q1581 has occurrences from 2026-10-05, Q1631 is one Mac event on /login; Q1615 functionsfetcherror stays separate: its events carry a signed-in user). Agents may not release or refund a non-test job, so it needs the owner: mark it done from both accounts (pays the admin account) or have it refunded. Then an admin taps Mark Reviewed on it in Admin > Stuck Jobs (resolve_stalled_job_flag): that is the ONLY writer of job_completion_nudges.resolved_at (measured in pg_proc), and both alerts' close rule (admin_queue_still_pending 'stalled-job') waits for it, not for the job's status.

The hard part is money: is_seed alone does not keep these rows away from auto-release-payment, process-scheduled-payouts, void-cancelled-payments, auto-resolve-disputes (a durable dispute would be auto-resolved and try a Stripe refund), charge-recurring-visits, auto-expire-jobs and money-reconciliation. That needs a dedicated fixture marker that every money cron and the reconciliation skip, a guard that derives the money crons and requires the skip, and an lh-money-escrow review. It is too large to start at the end of this session. Next: build it as one money-reviewed lane. **MERGED IN 2026-10-06 (triage):** Q941 (the 7 interruptions.spec.ts apply tests skip until the open un-applied escrow job exists; prod-audit.yml runs no seeder; still 7 skips on run 37270491218) and Q1076 (seed producers bypass accept_job/respond_to_direct_offer and leave states real jobs never reach; per the same 2026-10-04 decision they stamp states realistically). **MERGED IN 2026-10-06 (triage, second pass):** Q856 (shell-spacing reports messages@320-430 UNMEASURED until poster-e2e has two Active threads on live jobs). Q593 (a11y-prod's RESERVED accepted fixture) is the same build but stays its own item: it now mirrors nightly-red #2375. **DESIGN 2026-10-06 (e2e-reds lane; owner re-confirmed ONE permanent paid is_seed job, never public; lead: post-launch, both reviews before landing):** measured on prod 2026-10-06: anon open_jobs_browse = [] and 0 open/accepted/in_progress jobs with payment_status escrow (119 open abandoned seeds, 1 open unpaid, 1 accepted unpaid), so a11y-prod job-detail-1 skips UNJUSTIFIED (6 in run 37403676136) and prod-audit 37521973791 fails 16 tests + 7 unjustified skips on 'no in-progress job between the shared accounts'. (a) VISIBILITY: seed_jobs_hidden_publicly() is false on prod (feature_flags key unset), so an escrow seed would show on public Browse today (prod-seed.mjs:534 refuses escrow seeds for this reason), and flipping it true would also hide the row from helper-e2e. Migration: seed jobs visible only to viewers whose profiles.is_seed is true, on all four browse surfaces (open_jobs_browse, get_ranked_open_jobs, get_open_jobs_for_map, get_public_open_jobs): lh-authz-rls review. (b) UNHIREABLE: an escrow row with no PaymentIntent that press-every-control or a journey could apply to, hire and release would make release-payout transfer from the LIVE platform balance (no source_transaction). A fixture marker that apply_to_job / accept_application / release paths and every money cron listed above refuse, plus a guard deriving the money crons: lh-money-escrow review. (c) a service-role ensure script run by each workflow's fixtures job; a11y-prod authedScreens and the prod-audit gap lookups read the fixture id instead of open_jobs_browse. **BUILT 2026-10-07 (proof lane, owner decision 2026-10-07: ONE durable is_seed job funded in the database only, visible only to the test accounts, applied to and withdrawn from by 01-browse):** migration 20261007122020 adds public.test_fixture_jobs (server-only) and trg_jobs_test_fixture_never_hired, which for a listed job refuses, for every role, a Helpr, a direct offer, any status but open/cancelled and a payment_status move while open (PGlite src/test/pglite/testFixtureJobsCannotBeHired.pglite.mjs: 12 FAILED without it, ALL PASS with it, 3x replay); scripts/e2e/browseFixture.mjs keeps exactly one (ends an unusable open one before unregistering it); e2e/journeys/01-browse.spec.ts ensures it, applies, withdraws and cleans up in a finally. Guard: src/test/testFixtureJobsCannotBeHired.test.ts (6 @mutate, each red). lh-money-escrow review: SAFE WITH FIXES, both closed: (1) an unusable open fixture is ended before it is unregistered; (2) CI's poster is poster-e2e (71c56dfb), measured read-only: all 28 '[E2E DO NOT ACCEPT]' jobs created inside e2e-journeys 37552757280 + 37554735234's windows (00:35-01:47Z) and all 42 since 00:34Z are owned by 71c56dfb, none by 96c9899e; locally getSession('poster') mints poster-e2e too, so CI and local keep the same fixture. Still open in this item: the other suite states (accepted, in progress, completed, dispute: prod-audit / a11y / press), the /jobs:id + /user:id stall, press self-heal and the push 'Not Now' signal. **STATE FIXTURES BUILT 2026-10-12 (lane seed-fixtures, branch ready/seed-fixtures), applied to prod the same day:** scripts/e2e/seedStateFixtures.mjs (`--apply` idempotent, `--verify` read-only, `--teardown`, `--only=<key>`) keeps these poster-e2e jobs, is_seed, funded in the database only (escrow, no PaymentIntent, no Checkout Session), helper-e2e hired through the app's own RPCs: helper-scheduled-4d a87934d0-bb91-5215-b509-d49744cb2c5a and helper-scheduled-daybefore 9cd3fa38-7e53-57ca-813b-71bc15c8e389 (accepted; each with a 2-message thread; for Q1554, Q1561, Q593, Q1427), helper-arrived-photo 90aa6c8b-bf01-5430-ab29-ac182087ad5e (photo required, GPS-verified arrival, no before photo; Q1562), helper-completed c975aec8-ef5a-5fca-bac1-237320e7e557 (completed, payment_status released in the DB only; Q1561, work record), dispute-refiled a690c95f-ecd8-5791-86ce-b21b0c797674 (disputed, Helpr answered, withdrawn, re-filed; Q1327), crew-unconfirmed 0f2f7753-9390-57d1-8c4c-fcc84259bad3 (booked crew of helper-e2e + eli, neither confirmed; Q1402), and wipe row 2edc2b13-de6b-5d52-9528-c5fc168e4d81 in pre_verification_wipes for the incomplete account 60416646 (storage_done_at set, so the wipe sweep skips it; Q1286). Every child row id (applications, disputes, roster, messages) is printed by the script. NOT listed in test_fixture_jobs (its trigger refuses every status move, and a refusal inside a cron's set-based sweep would roll the sweep back for real users); the money safety is that every money mover refuses a job with no PaymentIntent before calling Stripe, the money crons skip is_seed, and no test account has a Stripe account. prod-seed.mjs never reviews or disputes them. So prod GoTrue has CAPTCHA ON for password sign-in: a script without a solved captcha never reaches the password check, which is the server-side backstop the client lockout needed. GoTrue's own per-IP sign-in limit could NOT be measured (the captcha check answers first; solving captchas from a script is not possible). Evidence ~/.lh-shots/sec-a-q1121/probe-20261015003509.json. Proposed guard (not built): a nightly live check that a captcha-less password sign-in still answers captcha_failed, so turning captcha off goes red.

Measured from code, not device: (a) on iOS `getLaunchUrl()` returns `ApplicationDelegateProxy.lastURL` and the cold-start `appUrlOpen` is retained until a listener attaches (node_modules/@capacitor/app AppPlugin.swift `retainUntilConsumed: true`), so a cold start from a link ran the handler TWICE (two Browser.close(), two navigate()); now the pair is collapsed (same URL, other source, within 10 s), while two real taps still route twice; (b) the appUrlOpen listener sat at the END of useNativePushSetup's one try, behind six push awaits, so any push throw left the process with no deep links; it now starts first on its own chain. The `cancelled`/effect re-run mechanism NB-017 hypothesised was already removed by the Q82 fix (NAVIGATE_REF, 9a06e04ce). AppDelegate.swift application(_:open:options:) and (_:continue:restorationHandler:) both forward to ApplicationDelegateProxy (read, correct; no UIScene manifest in Info.plist, so these are the entry points); Info.plist registers the `helpr` scheme. ANDROID: there is no `android/` project in the repo, so no intent-filter or launchMode exists to check — Android deep links cannot work until one is generated (`npx cap add android`) and given a `helpr` intent-filter + App Links; not done here. - [ ] **Q1415 LOW Ban-review loose ends (lh-money-escrow + lh-authz-rls reviews of fbdfa47c7, 2026-10-06; Q1324 follow-up).** (a) A tip held by the review never gets the aged-out notice the payout-hold path sends (auto-tip-charge skips before that branch). (b) Release during a review answers 'Failed to update the job status, please try again' forever (trg_refuse_payout_pending_during_ban_review); give it neutral final copy. (c) Refunds to a poster under review (cancel_escrow, void-cancelled-payments) and money-in paths (create-boost-payment, claim-gift-card) are not refused; none pays the account under review, decide whether any should be. (d) An open review is not itself a payout block: if an admin releases the Q1324 system hold (or an admin hold pre-empted it), instant-payout, cash-out-credits and Stripe auto payouts read only payout_holds; make checkPayoutHold count an open review, or have admin_release_payout_hold refuse while one is open. (e) PRE-EXISTING, the larger one (lh-money-escrow re-review of f2644839e): a late cancellation pays a Helpr under review behind only the payout hold, and charges the poster. poster_cancel_job has no ban/review check and still charges the fee (cancellation_fee_percent(true, 1) = 50, measured live) and strikes the poster; void-cancelled-payments (single job and crew share), heldTipRepay and the chargeback pay-back claim through cancellation_fee_transfers or Stripe directly and check checkPayoutHold only. Fix: no fee and no strike when the Helpr (or a crew member) is under an open review or banned; a refusal trigger on cancellation_fee_transfers like refuse_payout_claim_while_held (or (d) above covers all hold-only paths at once). (f) A retained-ban user_bans row written during a review has banned_by = self, and the confirm (UPDATE user_bans ... WHERE banned_by = p_user_id) rewrites it to its own judgment, which can weaken a stricter identity judgment; the confirm should apply the strictest judgment recorded during the review (lh-authz-rls re-review of f2644839e). finding: lh-money-escrow@fbdfa47c7#4 **PARTLY FIXED 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-4): (b) and (d) done.** (d) admin_release_payout_hold refuses 'ban_review_open' while the Helpr's review is open (20261013000532; PGlite old RED / new GREEN 3x; class guard src/test/payoutHoldReleaseWaitsForBanReview.test.ts: every function that clears payout_holds refuses an open review or is the review's own decision), so every hold-only path (instant-payout, cash-out-credits, Stripe auto payouts, and the late-cancel fee paths of (e)) stays blocked until the review decides; the admin copy says so. (b) a release refused by trg_refuse_payout_pending_during_ban_review answers neutral, final copy (create-payment edge case). (a) DONE too: auto-tip-charge ages out a tip held by an open review past its 14-day window like a payout hold (recorded 'failed', the poster told once, in words that never name the review; edge cases in src/test/edge/banReviewFreezesMoney.test.ts). Review copy: the hold-release refusal says a lift releases the hold and a confirmed ban keeps it (lh-money-escrow review #11; a confirmed-banned account's system hold can still be released by an admin, as before). STILL OPEN: (c) owner decision (refunds/money-in for a poster under review), (e) the poster is still charged the fee and struck when the Helpr is under review or banned (their pay itself now waits on the hold), **(f) DONE 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-5):** ban_review_retained_floor (20261013001155) returns the strictest standing retained judgment applied during the review (auto-applied, uncleared, a live retained row, recorded or re-applied since the review opened, not a spent suspension; a no-end suspension outlasts a dated one, in any row order), and BOTH admin_confirm_ban_settlement and lift_ban_settlement_review take it as a floor (a lift clears the card/bank matches first). PGlite src/test/pglite/banReviewConfirmStrictest.pglite.mjs on prod's bodies (md5 checked): live 6 FAIL, fixed ALL PASS 3x; guard src/test/banReviewConfirmKeepsStrictest.test.ts (every review decision that writes the standing takes the floor first, and the floor's filters). lh-authz-rls re-review: clean, with three LOW follow-ups kept here: (f1) the 'reapplied since' branch reads retained_bans.reapplied_at, stamped for ANY account, so a pardoned match whose retained row survived the unban (retained_via 'deletion', 1 on prod) can count if someone else re-applies that row during the review; fix: stamp the match row itself in enforce_retained_ban's ON CONFLICT flip and drop that branch; (f2) PRE-EXISTING: a lift whose result is 'banned' writes 'banned' over the review's 'banned', so no settlement trigger fires and the account's jobs stay unsettled once the freeze ends (a permanently_banned floor settles as of now, not as of the review); fix: run confirm's explicit settlement when a lift ends banned; (f3) PRE-EXISTING: confirm's rung branch still uses COALESCE(original_expires_at, '-infinity'), so a dated rung can replace a no-end review suspension. Also: the admin console should show the status a lift returns (a lift can now keep an identity ban). finding: lh-authz-rls@df48cf219#1

- [ ] **Q1714 MEDIUM An exact retained-ban phone is an UNVERIFIED number, so a banned account can poison someone else's number (lh-authz-rls review of lane3/q1586, 2026-10-12).** Phones are typed, never confirmed by SMS. A member who saves another person's real number and then gets banned leaves that number in retained_bans; when that person later adds it (sign-up or, since Q1586, any profile phone change) they are auto-banned at once (enforce_retained_ban), where before Q1586 a later add was only an admin doubt-check. Same exposure the sign-up path always had. Options for the owner: verify a phone (OTP) before it counts as a ban key or before it is matched, or keep exact matches on UNVERIFIED numbers as a doubt-check. finding: lh-authz-rls@d1438994d#1 **NOTE 2026-10-14 (owner, Q1823):** the app no longer asks for a phone anywhere a member goes on the way in: the prompt before the first Apply or Post was removed, and Complete Profile no longer has a phone field (owner, ~3 PM CT). A phone now enters only through Profile's optional field, so fewer numbers reach retained_bans; this item's question is otherwise unchanged.

- [ ] **Q1727 MEDIUM The tax preview and the charge use different addresses (lh-money-escrow review of Q1578, 2026-10-12).** The preview prices the job ZIP (CheckoutStep.tsx ~166 -> calculate-tax ~120); Checkout taxes the poster's BILLING address (create-payment ~850 customer_update.address auto, no shipping address). Now that assembly/handyman are taxed (Q1578), a poster billed in another parish pays that parish's rate, and one billed out of state pays $0 with no alert (taxedZeroOnTaxableLouisianaLabor only fires for LA billing). Fix: give Stripe the job site as the tax address (Stripe prefers a shipping address), so preview == charge. GUARD when built: a create-payment edge case where billing != job ZIP and the session carries the job-site address, red first. finding: lh-money-escrow@6e679c20c#1 **TRIED 2026-10-12 (lane 1 money), NOT landed: the first design is unsafe.** Measured in Stripe test mode: a Customer's `shipping` beats its billing address for Checkout tax (billing Austin TX + shipping Delcambre LA taxed Louisiana). But writing the job site onto the shared Stripe Customer persists: Subscriptions and invoices also tax `customer.shipping` first, so a Pro/Plus subscription (txcd_10103001, taxable SaaS) and every renewal would be taxed at the last job site, and a ZIP outside Louisiana stamped as state LA may make Stripe refuse every automatic-tax checkout on that customer (customer_tax_location_invalid; unmeasured). Options for the next build: (a) Checkout without automatic_tax, with a precomputed "Sales tax" line from a tax.calculations call at the job site, committed with createFromCalculation on completion (what recurring visits already do); or (b) shipping_address_collection so the poster confirms the job address in Checkout. Either way, validate the ZIP is 700xx-714xx and send the same address shape to the preview (Q1657). **TRIED AGAIN 2026-10-12 (branch ready/todo-a-4), dropped before landing:** the build saved the job ZIP as customer.shipping before the session, i.e. the unsafe first design above; the lh-money-escrow review blocked it on the same grounds (create-pro-checkout taxes the same Customer with automatic_tax, so a Pro/Plus checkout and every renewal would carry the last job site; two overlapping checkouts can tax one job at the other's site). The next build is option (a) or (b) above.

(lh-money-escrow review of Q417 (c), 2026-10-12; filed on the WIP branch as "Q1733", renumbered: main's Q1733 is the time picker).** OWNER DECISION 2026-10-12 (pop-up): YES. When Louisiana Helpr (admin, service or platform path, NOT the poster) ends a recurring series, paid visits with nobody booked get a FULL refund, card fee included; a poster ending it themselves keeps today's rule (Q808, fee withheld). BUILT (branch ready/items-a-series-refund-r2): 20261012233226_platform_series_end_refunds_in_full adds the server-only public.series_platform_ends (RLS on, no policies, REVOKE FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated), written by end_recurring_series_by_platform ONLY for kind 'admin' (a chargeback end is the poster's own bank dispute, so it keeps Q808; a series the poster already ended answers already_ended and records nothing); the admin end's notice says "refunded in full". charge-recurring-visits reads the row in the stale-visit sweep (withholdFee is false for it, even on an unheld date or a tagged intent; an unreadable row refunds nothing that run and retries) and in the mid-run series_ended refusal (refunds in full and tells the poster; an unreadable row keeps the fee withheld and pages with the by-hand step). NOT covered (corrected 2026-10-12 after the lh-money-escrow review of 389a4d210, #1): a series ended by a permanent BAN of the Helpr (end_series_for_banned_account) still withholds the card fee (its notices say "less the card processing fee", _shared/seriesRefund.ts), and so does a chargeback end; whether either should refund in full is the owner question Q1763. PROOF: src/test/pglite/platformSeriesEndRefundsInFull.pglite.mjs ALL PASS 3x, 5 FAIL with NEW_MIGRATION=skip. REVIEW FIXES (lh-money-escrow of 6b2434f20, #4): an unreadable platform-end record mid-run no longer tells the poster "less the card fee"; the sweep no longer settles a platform-ended visit refunded only less the fee (it pages ops to refund the rest); a test pins that the intent's fee-withheld tag is ignored once an admin ended the series. The table's column is end_kind, CHECK (end_kind IN ('admin')). GUARDS: src/test/seriesEndByPlatform.test.ts (@mutate on the admin-only INSERT, the table REVOKE and the in-full copy) and src/test/edge/charge-recurring-visits.test.ts (eight Q1735 cases, @mutate on withholdFee, owesTheFee and platformEndedMidRun). (Retired 2026-10-14 with the 10-12 rule: its checks were that series_platform_ends exists and that end_recurring_series_by_platform writes it, plus src/test/seriesEndByPlatform.test.ts; the new checks are on the sub-line below.) finding: lh-money-escrow@60cd5ff79#1 REVIEW FIX 2026-10-12 (lh-money-escrow of 389a4d210, #2): an on-session PAID row refused mid-run while series_platform_ends cannot be read is left 'paid' (not settled), so the stale-visit sweep reads the record again and settles it or pages until the fee is refunded (guard: charge-recurring-visits.test.ts, shown red on the old line, @mutate). Gaps left: Q1766.

- [ ] **Q1736 LOW Spent does not count the crew block fees captured from an uncaptured hold (lh-money-escrow review of Q1345, 2026-10-12).** void-cancelled-payments' fee-capture branch captures the cancellation fee PLUS the crew's block fees (crew_block_fees), but posterSpend counts that path as jobs.cancellation_fee capped at the charge, so a crew poster's Spent misses the block fees that left their card. Fix: posterSpend adds the job's paid/owed crew_block_fees on this path (or the cron stamps the full capture somewhere Spent reads). GUARD when built: a posterSpend case with a block fee, and a void-cancelled-payments crew case asserting stamp == (captured - blockCents)/100. finding: lh-money-escrow@7ef117d25#4 (Re-filed 2026-10-12 by the proof4 lane: commit b4dc2f1d3 lost this item's header and merged its text into Q1663.)
- [ ] **Q1737 MEDIUM A missing /assets/ or /fonts/ file now 404s, but the 404 carries `Cache-Control: public, max-age=31536000, immutable` (found proving Q1720, 2026-10-12).** MEASURED with curl -sI on www (before 21:33Z, build c4b1b5254): /assets/missing-Q1663-x.js, /assets/notificationFeed-B4je1ZJb.js and /fonts/missing-x.woff2 all answer 404 text/plain with that header, because vercel.json's `/assets/(.*)` and `/fonts/(.*)` header rules stamp every response under those paths, found or not. A browser may keep a 404 with explicit freshness, so a chunk asked for in a deploy's propagation window (Q199) could stay a cached 404 under that URL. Not reproduced in a browser. Fix to weigh: limit the immutable header to files that exist (e.g. `missing` on the header rule, or a 404 page with no-store). GUARD when built: extend src/test/immutablePathsNeverServeTheSpa.test.ts, plus a live curl in a post-deploy check.
- [ ] **Q1739 LOW The saved-only feed and the saved count disagree when a saved job is not on the loaded pages or is not shown at all (lh-silent-failure review of Q1701, 2026-10-12, code read, not measured on prod).** savedJobIds (the saved count on the bookmark, Dashboard.tsx savedCount) holds every saved id, while the saved-only feed shows only saved jobs that are on a page already loaded AND pass every feed cull. A saved job on a later page, closed or filled, filtered out, applied to, dismissed, or from a blocked poster counts but never shows, so the count can say 3 while the feed says "Nothing saved yet". A load-more-until-found fix was tried and withdrawn in the Q1701 branch: it pages through the whole feed for jobs that will never show, and useLoadMoreSentinel only re-attaches on a dependency change, so the auto-paging did not start on its own. Needs: a server read of "saved and still shown to me" for both the count and the saved-only list (or the saved-only list read by id). GUARD when built: a test that the count and the saved-only list agree for a saved job on page 3 and for a saved closed job. finding: lh-silent-failure@28e0cfac5#2

- [ ] **Q1755 MEDIUM A test account can apply to (and be hired on) a REAL job, which puts a test account on real money (found by the lh-authz-rls and lh-money-escrow reviews of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12; the 2026-10-09 incident was exactly this: the shared test Helpr applied to the owner's real job).** enforce_application_job_state rule C6 only stops a REAL user applying to a seed job (`v_job.is_seed AND seed_hidden_for(helper)`); nothing stops the reverse, nor a direct offer or accept between a real poster and a test account. With test mode for test accounts the money side is safe (a payout of live escrow to a test Helpr refuses as mixed_parties, and the poster's refund still runs in live mode), but such a job can never be paid out and needs a refund. Writers measured on prod before locking (read-only SQL, 2026-10-15): only redeem_gift_card assigns it in pg_proc, and its EXECUTE is postgres + service_role only; edge functions write it with the service role; src/ and the 7119 build (c16b0f0dc) only read it, so the review freeze holds. - [~] **Q1759 LOW charge-recurring-visits settles money ALREADY taken (a paid visit's refund, an open visit Checkout's expiry) as NEW money, so a mixed series is skipped instead of refunded (found by the lh-money-escrow review of the test-mode branch, 2026-10-12; 0 recurring_visit_payments rows on prod, measured by the review).** The stale/series-over loop takes its client with kind "new" from the parent's parties plus the visit's payer and Helpr (charge-recurring-visits/index.ts ~734-742), and uses it both to expire an open visit Checkout and to refund a PAID visit. That breaks the rule in _shared/stripeMode.ts (money already taken runs where it sits; a refund needs only the payer): a real payer's series with a test Helpr on a visit (the Q1755 entry path) never auto-refunds its live money (a defect every run) and never expires an open live Checkout. The mode also comes from the parties, not the visit row's own cs_live_/cs_test_ session, and create-payment's recurring_visit mode counts only the parent's parties while the cron also counts the visit's Helpr, so a visit Checkout can be minted in a mode the cron then refuses. Fix: decide expire and refund with kind "refund" from the visit row's own session prefix. Money change: lh-money-escrow pass. GUARD when built: an edge test of a mixed series whose paid visit is refunded on the payer's key. **STRANDED WIP 2026-10-14 (stranded2-1013 lane):** an unreviewed build covering Q1759, Q1760, Q1761 and the fully gift-funded job's late-cancellation fee (its commit says "WIP for review", lane A2, 2026-10-12) sits on local claim/Q1781 = origin wip/stranded2-1013-q1781-wip @ 80d2480f2. Start from it and give it its lh-money-escrow pass; it was not landed because the owner ordered no new to-do work during the 1.0.4 release (lead, 2026-10-13). finding: lh-money-escrow@000ca9c1e#2 **BUILT 2026-10-15 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-10; rebuilt from the stranded WIP 80d2480f2):** the stale/series-over loop in charge-recurring-visits decides an open visit Checkout's expiry and a PAID visit's refund with visitMoneyClient: the visit row's own Checkout Session prefix (cs_live_/cs_test_) where the money sits, else its payer alone (a refund needs only the payer); a cs_test_ row of a real payer stays on the live key, as jobMoneyMode decides for a job. The visit's Helpr and the series' parties no longer count, so a real payer's series with a test Helpr on a visit is refunded live instead of skipped as a mix, and a visit Checkout that create-payment minted from the parent's parties is no longer refused by the cron. After the lh-money-escrow review: a cs_live_ row needs no test-account read (a failed read never holds back a live refund), and a failed read on a row with no prefix fails closed (a defect, no refund). The payer alone decides a cs_test_ row (jobMoneyMode would count every job party): only sandbox money can land on the wrong key, where it is skipped, never moved. GUARD src/test/edge/charge-recurring-visits.test.ts (3 Q1759 cases, 2 @mutate each shown red; 2 of the 3 FAIL on main's source, all pass after). Left for the review round: lh-money-escrow review on the landed sha. done-when: test src/test/edge/charge-recurring-visits.test.ts

GUARD when built: the twin's own run, recorded in src/test/nullArgNeverAllows.test.ts's header.

Fix: keep the Apple refresh token at sign-in (server side), and have delete-own-account call /auth/revoke with a client secret signed by the Sign in with Apple key. Needs that key from the owner. - [ ] **Q1802 LOW A recurring-series date a Helpr held BEFORE their saved birthday became under 18 is still booked by the cron (lh-authz-rls review of 30e7034c4, Q1586(c); 0 under-18 birthdays on prod 2026-10-12, so unreachable today).** Q1586(c) refuses every NEW series_visit_holds row for an under-18 member, but a hold that already exists (claimed while the birthday was empty or adult, then an admin corrects it) is still turned into a visit by charge-recurring-visits: it charges the poster and inserts the visit with helper_id set (a service insert, not judged), then the applications upsert is refused by trg_applications_refuse_under_18 and the cron pages ops 'Recurring visit created without its application row'. Fix: before charging, skip and release a hold whose holder member_under_18 (service_role may call it) and tell the poster the date is open again, never the age. GUARD when built: a charge-recurring-visits edge case with an under-18 holder: no charge, the hold released. finding: lh-authz-rls@30e7034c4#2
- [ ] **Q1803 MEDIUM OWNER DECISION: what happens when a member types a birthday under 13? (lh-authz-rls re-review of 44ca75e95, 2026-10-13; a compliance question, not authz).** Since Q1586(c)'s fix (owner 2026-10-13: a typed under-18 birthday is SAVED and Apply/Post grey out), the birthday wheels in PhotoPrompt and CompleteProfile go up to today, so a birthday under 13 can be saved, and the account is kept with its name, phone and address. That is actual knowledge that a child under 13 is using the app (COPPA). Options for the owner: keep the account greyed out like any minor; or close and delete an under-13 account on save; or refuse to keep the personal details until a parent is involved. Measured: 0 under-18 birthdays on prod 2026-10-12 (the reviewer's read). finding: lh-authz-rls@44ca75e95#5 **NOTE 2026-10-14 (owner, Q1823):** no screen asks a birthday now: PhotoPrompt is gone and Complete Profile's birthday wheel was replaced by the 18+ box (owner, ~3 PM CT). A birthday reaches the database only through complete-signup's dateOfBirth (no current client sends it) or an existing saved row, so today the question has no member-facing entry point; it applies again if a birthday field returns.
- [ ] **Q1804 LOW The unauthenticated sign-up completion path can now SAVE an under-18 birthday on a brand-new account, which its owner cannot clear (lh-authz-rls re-review of 44ca75e95, 2026-10-13).** complete-signup's bodyUserId path (only within 30 minutes of account creation, never signed in, bio still empty) used to answer an under-18 birthday with a 403; since 44ca75e95 it saves it, and lock_under_18_birthday plus complete-signup's rewrite refusal keep it, so someone who learned a brand-new account's id could lock it out of Apply and Post until support corrects it. The same caller could already write an adult birthday and other fields through that path, and a new account's id is hard to learn. Fix: on the bodyUserId (no session) path, ignore a birthday entirely (a birthday comes only from a signed-in member). GUARD when built: a complete-signup edge test that a no-session call with an under-18 birthday writes no date_of_birth. finding: lh-authz-rls@44ca75e95#3
- [ ] **Q1805 HIGH Land the held phone half of Q1586(a) once Apple has decided on 1.0.4 (split out of a2-1u by the lead, 2026-10-14).** Branch ready/phone-save-after-review (listed in ~/.lh-landq/queue.hold-ready.txt) holds 20261013012700_phone_save_rpc_rate_limited.sql (save_my_phone, the rate-limited phone save; REVOKE UPDATE (phone) and INSERT on profiles from anon and authenticated; client_compat_floor() 2), src/lib/phoneSave.ts, CLIENT_COMPAT_EPOCH 2 and the screens' saveMyPhone calls. Build 7119 still saves the phone with profiles.update, so while it is with Apple this would show the reviewer a refused save or 'Update Helpr'. Until it lands, a member can still probe whether a phone number is on another account with no rate limit. Land it only after the freeze flag in release/ says frozen: false. GUARD: src/test/appReviewFreeze.test.ts fails it while the freeze holds; phoneSavedOnlyThroughRpc.test.ts and phoneSaveRpc.pglite.mjs come with it. finding: lh-authz-rls@d3a9253aa#1
- [ ] **Q1769 LOW A decided dispute on a Stripe TEST-mode payment can never be settled, even after it is re-opened (lh-money-escrow review of Q1732, 2026-10-12; 0 such disputes on prod, measured by the review).** execute-dispute-split refuses a test-mode escrow ("this escrow was paid in Stripe test mode", "this job's checkout ran in Stripe test mode"), so every decision on such a job fails again; Q1732's button re-opens one only when test-mode refund/transfer records exist, and Retry settlement loops. rpc_settle_dispute_without_payment closes only a job with NO payment on file. Needs an owner decision: close such a dispute as "test mode, nothing real moved" (a sibling of close-without-payment that requires the escrow to be checked at Stripe as a test object, audited), or leave them for hand review. Note (2026-10-12): once ready/stripe-test-mode-r (Stripe test mode for test accounts) lands, execute-dispute-split runs a job whose every party is a test account on the TEST key, so a decision on such a job can settle; this item then narrows to test-mode escrow on a job that is NOT all test accounts (pre-go-live sandbox payments). GUARD when built: a PGlite proof plus an edge test of the Stripe check. finding: lh-money-escrow@6b2434f20#3
  - Merged 2026-10-14 (same problem): Q1738 is ticked into this item and keeps its full text. From Q1738: a decided or superseded dispute on a TEST-mode escrow can never close; it needs an owner decision or an admin close-out for test-mode objects. Unreachable today (0 disputes on prod when measured).
- [ ] **Q1764 LOW A dispute split already running when an admin's Clear test-mode records commits can overwrite the cleared marker (lh-money-escrow review of 389a4d210, 2026-10-12; a window of milliseconds between the clear and the re-open).** execute-dispute-split reads the cleared marker (execution_error starting "test-mode records cleared by an admin") only at its first dispute read; the re-read inside the settlement claim does not select execution_error, and a run that refuses after the clear calls markFailed, which overwrites the marker. With the marker gone and no ledger ids left, the button then answers "nothing to clear" and supersede has to be pressed again. Fix: re-check the marker in the claim's re-read and never let markFailed overwrite it. GUARD when built: an edge test where the clear lands between the first read and the claim, asserting no split and the marker kept. finding: lh-money-escrow@389a4d210#3
- [ ] **Q1806 LOW A fully gift-funded job's late-cancellation fee is never paid to the Helpr (found doing Q1490, 2026-10-12; code read, not seen on prod: 0 redeemed gift cards on a job).** void-cancelled-payments prices the fee (computeCancellationFee, or the crew shares) BEFORE it resolves the PaymentIntent, but a fully gift-funded job has none and takes the 'no payment found' branch: it settles payment_status 'cancelled' with the whole gift restored and pays no fee, and Parts D/E only retry jobs whose payment_status is 'refunded' with the fee 'charged'. So a Helpr (or crew) cancelled on late is owed a fee nobody pays. Fix to weigh: pay the fee from the gift (restore the gift less the fee, as Q1490 does for block fees, and pay it from the platform balance with a ledger row), with the same no-fee rules as a card-paid job. GUARD when built: a void-cancelled-payments edge case for a gift-funded single job and crew with a late cancel. The same class, from the lh-money-escrow review of Q1490 (#9): a crew paid partly by gift and partly by card whose card capture is smaller than its block fees still restores the whole gift (settleCancelledJob on the card branches), and Part F pays the rest of the fee from the platform balance. finding: lh-money-escrow@e45c44f44#9
- [x] **Q1782 LOW Two refund-notice gaps left after the parallel fix of the sweep double-send and the failed-refund tax page (found doing those items, 2026-10-12; the same fixes as ready/items-c-refund-notice, PR #2918, were also built on ready/todo-a-1, which is superseded and not landed).** (1) The webhook-first order: when charge.refunded announces a Stripe-listed release as 'Refund issued' BEFORE void-cancelled-payments writes its release notice, the release is still announced too (the same money twice); a fix built and reviewed clean on origin/ready/todo-a-1 (commit 'a re-issued refund gets its own tax reversal; a release a refund notice already announced is not told twice'): sendRefundNotice(kind release) skips when a finished refund:<re> notice for the same payment and amount exists within an hour, failing open on a read error. (2) A full refund re-issued after a failed one reuses the tax reversal's idempotency key and reference refund:<charge>, so Stripe returns the old reversal and nothing is reversed or paged; the same branch keyed the reversal on the refund id instead (#2918 instead tells ops to leave the reversal; check which rule the owner wants). GUARD when built: the refundNotice and stripe-webhook edge cases on that branch. finding: lh-money-escrow@4dfdc0a85#1 **STATUS 2026-10-15 (sec-a, read from git):** part (1) is ON MAIN as 7ce549414 ("a release the webhook already announced as a refund of the same money is not announced again"; guard: two refundNotice edge cases in src/test/edge/refundNotice.test.ts, lh-money-escrow clean in its trailer). Part (2) is NOT built and is a rule choice, not a missing fix: main's pageVisitTaxForFailedRefund tells ops to LEAVE the reversal on a re-issued refund (1b0f31114), while ready/todo-a-1 (2a6f3754d, superseded, recorded in stranded-accepted) keyed a fresh reversal on the refund id; 7ce549414's message records that the two designs conflict (review finding #8). Left: the lead (or the owner, if it is theirs) picks one rule for part (2); then build its guard and tick. **DONE 2026-10-15 (sec-a, ready/sec-a-11): part (2) built on the rule the lead ruled and the owner decided (pop-up 2026-10-15 ~3:30 AM UTC: tax stays paid on fees we keep; reversed only on money returned to the poster).** reverseVisitTaxForRefund (stripe-webhook/handlers/_visitTaxReversal.ts) now reverses each LIVE refund once, keyed on the refund id (reference refund:<re_id>, idempotency key visit-tax-reversal:<re_id>), recorded on the PaymentIntent as metadata.tax_rev_<re_id> so a redelivery skips it; a refund's share = min(its amount, the transaction's labor + tax left after the refunds before it), so the cumulative reversal never exceeds what was collected and a kept late-cancel / crew block fee keeps its share collected; a refund of the whole labor + tax with nothing reversed yet is mode full, every other share a partial flat_amount reversal. A refund that later FAILS has its own reversal undone by a full reversal of that reversal transaction (undoVisitTaxForFailedRefund, charge.refund.updated; reference refund-failed:<re_id>), replacing the LEAVE-it page. GUARD: the 'recurring-visit sales tax reversal per refund' cases in src/test/edge/stripe-webhook.test.ts (full, retry, redelivery key, second partial, cap, kept late-cancel fee, re-issue after a failed refund, refused reversal paged with the hand-reversal record to set, a delivery whose every refund failed pages nothing, only a structured reference-taken refusal reads as done) and the two undo cases in src/test/edge/chargeRefundUpdated.test.ts; 11 red on origin/main's source, 9 @mutate lines each red. Reviews: lh-money-escrow approve and lh-silent-failure no blockers; their should-fixes (all-refunds-failed false page, text-only reference match, hand-reversal pages without the record to set, unrecorded-stamp path) are fixed in the same branch; the unproven Stripe behaviour (undoing a FULL reversal, then reversing again) is Q?visit-tax-testmode-proof.
- [ ] **Q1821 LOW MapKit's own script asked for WebAssembly and our CSP refused it (found 2026-10-13 re-measuring Q1716, read-only SQL).** error_logs source='csp': script-src blocked `wasm-eval` from https://cdn.apple-mapkit.com/ti/csr/1.x.x/mk-csr.js on www /home, twice (2026-10-12 18:00:42Z and 2026-10-13 21:38:42Z). vercel.json's script-src has no 'wasm-unsafe-eval'. - [ ] **Q1820 MEDIUM Nothing notices when the Stripe TEST-mode webhook endpoint stops delivering (found 2026-10-13: it had been off since 2026-10-06, measured read-only).** History: all three test-mode endpoints were disabled (we_1UNfFlKp2H4b7tECoGERXN18 pointed at a function that does not exist, .../stripe-webhook-testmode; we_1U8Abh... at stripe-webhook, disabled by Q839), so 0 of 31 jobs with a cs_test_ session had ever reached escrow and a paid test checkout on a cancelled job made void-cancelled-payments 500 every run (Q1809). Fixed at the source by the lead with owner approval on 2026-10-13: we_1UNfFl now points at /functions/v1/stripe-webhook, enabled, 14 events; measured 2026-10-14 07:15Z: 62 deliveries verified by STRIPE_TEST_WEBHOOK_SECRET since 20:57Z, 0 signature failures, 17 cs_test_ jobs past unpaid. Left (the prevention): stripe-webhook-config-check reads webhook_endpoints with the LIVE key only, so a disabled or mis-pointed TEST endpoint is invisible to stripe-webhook-guard.yml. - [ ] **Q1822 LOW In Playwright WebKit the admin Jump To palette is see-through over a second open dialog (found 2026-10-14 proving Q1786, measured).** prod 904f3f3a1, seed-admin at /admin, 375, with the Terms re-agree alertdialog open underneath (the seed admin had not re-agreed): in WebKit the page behind is not blurred, and the re-agree card's text shows through the palette's search field and list (~/.lh-shots/proof3-1014/out/q1786-palette-375-wk2.png, recorded as a defect). Chromium shows both blurred. Both engines compute the same styles: overlay blur(24px) saturate(1.5), dialogs blur(40px) over rgba(240,242,244,.95). WebKit reports CSS.supports("-webkit-backdrop-filter") false and the unprefixed one true. NOT measured: whether a real iPhone (WKWebView) shows the same. It may be the stacked-backdrop-filter paint class behind the Linux WebKit Filters stall (wip/proof2-1014-blur), or only a Playwright-WebKit screenshot limit. Next: look on the simulator with two dialogs open.
- [ ] **Q1824 MEDIUM The web app can go live before the migration it needs (lh-authz-rls review of the noprompt branch, 2026-10-14).** .github/workflows/prod-deploy.yml has no dependency on db-deploy.yml (its only needs: is [deploy]); measured with gh run list on one push 2026-10-14: prod-deploy finished 19:52:53Z, db-deploy 19:55:30Z, and earlier db-deploy runs took 13-15 min. A client that writes a column or calls an RPC a migration in the same merge adds fails on prod in that window (CLAUDE.md's PGRST202 fallback covers RPCs only, and a fallback cannot help a write the gate depends on, like Complete Profile's age_attested_at). Fix: make prod-deploy wait until every migration on the commit it ships is applied on prod (read supabase_migrations.schema_migrations, or needs: on a green db-deploy for that sha), with a guard that the dependency stays. finding: lh-authz-rls@9d35e9f18#1
- [ ] **Q1825 LOW write-contract's column_pending skips the grant check (lh-authz-rls review of the noprompt branch, 2026-10-14).** scripts/audit/write-contract.mjs now warns column_pending for a column a not-yet-applied migration adds, and continues before checking grants, so a pending migration that adds a column to a column-granted table (profiles, jobs) but forgets GRANT UPDATE (col) passes as a warning until the snapshot refresh after deploy. Fix: when the table grants per column, require a matching GRANT INSERT/UPDATE (...col...) in the pending migrations or reject no_column_grant; prove it red in src/test/writeContract.test.ts. finding: lh-authz-rls@9d35e9f18#3
- [ ] **Q1826 LOW OWNER DECISION: should deleting an account clear its 18+ box record? (lh-authz-rls review of the noprompt branch, 2026-10-14).** purge_user_data and wipe_pre_verification_account set date_of_birth to NULL but do not touch the new profiles.age_attested_at (a bare timestamp of when the member ticked "I confirm I am 18 years of age or older"). It is not personal data on an anonymised row, and it may be worth keeping as a legal record of the attestation. Options: keep it (say so beside both functions), or clear it (needs the pre-verification-wipe flag, which pin_age_attested_at already honours). finding: lh-authz-rls@9d35e9f18#5
- [ ] **Q1827 LOW A crew refund re-run can owe a few cents more than the refund it already made, and page ops for them (lh-money-escrow review of 79e9a6bb5, #3; code read, not measured).** Since 2026-10-14 process-scheduled-payouts returns a crew's fee share only above Stripe's card fee (crewFeeShareReturnCents), and that fee is the charge's real balance-transaction fee when it can be read, else the card-rate estimate. A run that used the estimate and a later run that reads a lower real fee compute different cents, so cardOwedCents rises above the refund already ledgered and the shortRefund branch pages for the difference; the comment "a re-run owes the same cents" is no longer strictly true. Also unverified (lh-silent-failure review of 79e9a6bb5, #7): if one crew charge could get BOTH a crew dispute refund and an unfilled-spot refund, each caps the fee share on its own and the two together could return part of the card fee. Fix: stamp the fee the first run used (refund metadata) and reuse it; check whether both refunds can hit one charge. GUARD when built: a process-scheduled-payouts-crew case of a re-run whose fee read changed, and one of a dispute plus an unfilled refund on one charge. finding: lh-money-escrow@79e9a6bb5#3
- [ ] **Q1828 LOW Every taxed recurring-visit refund that keeps the card fee is a PARTIAL refund at Stripe, so its sales-tax reversal is paged to ops by hand (lh-money-escrow review of 79e9a6bb5, #6; lh-silent-failure review of 79e9a6bb5, #5; code read).** stripe-webhook/handlers/_visitTaxReversal.ts reverses a visit's Stripe Tax transaction automatically only on a FULL refund; a partial one raises "Taxed recurring visit partly refunded: reverse its sales tax by hand". The never-absorb rule (owner, 2026-10-14) makes every fee-keeping visit refund partial, now including a series an admin ended (before, those were full and reversed automatically). Money is right; the tax report needs a person each time. Fix: reverse the refunded share automatically (Stripe Tax transactions.createReversal mode partial with the refunded amount), keyed on the refund id. Not checked by either reviewer: how Stripe Tax handles a partial refund of a Checkout automatic_tax JOB charge (admin full refunds are now partial too). GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook case of a fee-withheld visit refund reversing its tax share with no page. finding: lh-money-escrow@79e9a6bb5#6
- [ ] **Q1829 LOW A duplicate-funding refund whose gift restore fails is never retried: the redelivery skips the restore (lh-money-escrow re-review of dfae918ae, 2026-10-14).** checkoutSessionCompleted's duplicate-funding branch throws when the gift restore UPDATE errors ("The refund is keyed, so the retry this throw causes refunds nothing twice and tries the restore again"), but on Stripe's redelivery the gift is already 'redeemed' on this job, the consume step logs "Reserved gift card already consumed for this job, skipping" and leaves giftConsumedHere false, so the restore never runs and the gift stays spent on a job it did not fund (code read, not seen on prod). The Q1819 ledger write was moved after the restore for this reason (ready/sec-a-1). Fix: on the duplicate branch, restore when the gift is redeemed on this job by THIS checkout (session metadata gift_card_id), not only when this delivery consumed it. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case where the restore errors, then a redelivery asserts the gift is back to 'sent'. finding: lh-money-escrow@dfae918ae#1
- [ ] **Q?dup-fee-refund-after-cancel LOW A cancel that runs in the seconds between a checkout funding its job and settleOnboardingFee refunding a duplicate $2 setup fee returns those $2 twice (lh-money-escrow review of ba10093ae, Q1675 follow-up; code read, narrow window, not seen on prod).** checkoutSessionCompleted sets payment_status 'escrow' (~958), calls instant-job-match, and only then runs settleOnboardingFee (~1227); a cancel_escrow or void-cancelled-payments in that gap refunds the full ceiling (which still holds the duplicate $2), then settleOnboardingFee refunds the $2 again out of the card fee. Fix: settleOnboardingFee skips its refund (and says so) when the charge's amount_refunded already covers the escrow ceiling, or the duplicate refund runs before the escrow flip. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge test with a charge already refunded by a cancel. finding: lh-money-escrow@ba10093ae#3
- [ ] **Q?lost-dispute-checkout-tax LOW A lost chargeback on a Checkout-taxed charge (a one-off job from create-payment, or a recurring visit paid through settleRecurringVisitCheckout) may leave its sales tax reported as collected (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1724 fix, 2026-10-14; not checked against Stripe).** Q1724 reverses the Tax API transaction an off-session visit charge records itself; charges taxed by Checkout `automatic_tax` are not handled on charge.dispute.closed (lost). Unverified whether Stripe Tax reverses a Checkout-taxed charge's tax on its own when a dispute is lost (its docs say it does not for the PaymentIntent-linked Tax API flow). Fix when confirmed: check in Stripe test mode or the Stripe Tax docs; if not automatic, reverse it on the lost branch of chargeDisputeClosed the way the visit Tax API reversal there does. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case with a lost dispute on a Checkout-taxed charge. finding: lh-money-escrow@803a6c258#3 (review of a superseded duplicate build of Q1724, bundle ~/.lh-backups/sec-a3-superseded-dupes-2026-10-15.bundle; main's Q1724 also reverses only the Tax API transaction a visit charge records itself)
- [ ] **Q?crew-seat-schedule-race LOW A crew-seat accept or hire that runs at the same moment as a schedule-change accept can still double-book a Helpr (lh-authz-rls review of the Q1676 fix, 2026-10-15; code read on live definitions, not seen on prod).** Live, only respond_job_schedule_change takes the per-Helpr advisory lock `helpr_schedule:<helper>`; the crew-seat accept and hire paths do not, and its FOR SHARE on jobs rows does not stop a concurrent group_job_helpers insert, so a seat accepted in the same instant as a schedule change onto that time passes both checks. Older than Q1676 (not caused by it). Fix when built: the crew-seat accept / hire RPCs take the same advisory lock before their own clash check (or re-check after insert). GUARD when built: a PGlite race proof (two sessions, Promise.allSettled) that only one of the two lands. finding: lh-authz-rls@78c205c37#1
- [ ] **Q?job-hold-followups LOW Three loose ends of the Q1491 job payout hold (lh-money-escrow review of the Q1491 rebuild 7133adc73, 2026-10-15).** (a) The hold is upserted before the escrow write's funded-job guard, so a delivery that turns out to be a duplicate funding (refunded in full by that branch) leaves a hold on a properly funded job: fail-safe, but it blocks a legitimate payout and the "underfunded" page is wrong. Fix: upsert with `.select("job_id")` (a row only when this delivery inserted it) and delete that hold in the duplicate-funding branch. (b) money-reconciliation excludes only Helpr holds, so a job-held payout_pending job is reported as payout_pending_stranded: load job_payout_holds and report it as held. (c) process-scheduled-payouts finds a job hold only at the claim, so every run repeats the account and transfer-group reads and rolls back the onboarding fee for each held job: call checkJobPayoutHold beside the Helpr check and skip with PAYOUT_HELD_CODE. Also weigh: void-cancelled-payments' two fee transfers send unlinked (no source_transaction) when latest_charge is missing; the trigger DETAIL says a held job pays nobody. GUARD when built: a stripe-webhook edge case of a duplicate-funded delivery (no hold left), a money-reconciliation case (held, not stranded) and a process-scheduled-payouts case (no Stripe read for a held job). finding: lh-money-escrow@7133adc73#3
- [ ] **Q?test-stripe-id-on-real-row LOW No nightly check that a real (non-seed) row never holds a Stripe TEST-mode id (split from Q1761, 2026-10-15).** Q1761's fix keeps sandbox refund notices away from real people, but testEventScope still lets a test-mode event that names nobody through, on the assumption that only rows a test payment wrote hold its ids. Measured 2026-10-12 by the reviews (read-only SQL): 0 real jobs hold a cs_test_ session or a pre-go-live PaymentIntent. Nothing re-measures it. Fix: a nightly read-only check (prod-audit or the ops ledger) that counts non-seed jobs, recurring_visit_payments, payment_refunds, payout_transfers, gift_cards and tips (sendRefundNotice falls back to the last two for a recipient, and a test-mode full refund or dispute revokes a gift card by PaymentIntent; lh-money-escrow review of the Q1761 rebuild #4) holding a cs_test_/pi_ id minted before 2026-09-27 or a test Connect account, and pages on any. GUARD when built: a unit test of the check's query on a fixture with one such row (red) and none (green).
- [ ] **Q?fixture-scan-comment-quotes LOW fixtureSchemaContract's literal scanner reads apostrophes in comments as string quotes, so one apostrophe added to a test file's comment can make it grade the whole file as one table row (found building Q1782 (2), 2026-10-15).** objectLiterals/balanced (src/test/helpers/schemaConstraints.ts) respects quotes but not comments: in src/test/edge/chargeRefundUpdated.test.ts an unbalanced brace in a header @mutate line plus one comment apostrophe turned a 9,537-character span (every test in the file) into one "recurring_visit_payments row" whose first status was a job's 'cancelled', a false red. Worked around in that file by rewording two comments; the scanner still has the hole (a parity flip can as easily HIDE a real bad fixture). Fix: run the source through blankComments (src/test/helpers/blankNonCode.ts) before objectLiterals. Done when: the scanner blanks comments and a test proves an apostrophe in a comment no longer changes which literals are graded.
- [ ] **Q?visit-tax-testmode-proof LOW Prove in Stripe TEST mode that a FULL visit tax reversal can be undone and the visit reversed again (lh-money-escrow + lh-silent-failure reviews of Q1782 (2), 2026-10-15).** Q1782 (2) reverses a refund that returns the whole labor + tax with mode full (the lead's rule), and a refund that later fails undoes its reversal with a full reversal of that reversal transaction. Stripe documents undoing a PARTIAL reversal this way, not a full one, and nothing says a re-issued refund's own reversal is then accepted on the original (it may be refused as an over-reversal: that pages, so it is not silent, but the tax on money really returned would stay reported). Also capture Stripe's real error for a reused reversal reference (type, code, param, message) so referenceTaken() in stripe-webhook/handlers/_visitTaxReversal.ts matches it exactly, and the real idempotency-in-use error. Not done in the build: no Stripe test key is on this Mac and the live account must not get test tax transactions. Done when: one test-mode run (tax calculation, createFromCalculation, full reversal, full reversal of that reversal, a second reversal on the original) is recorded with its ids, and the test fixtures use the captured errors (or the code switches to partial flat_amount everywhere if Stripe refuses the cycle). finding: lh-money-escrow@994f077b2#1
- [ ] **Q?test-gift-refused-row-pending LOW A TEST-mode gift the webhook refuses (not test-to-test) leaves its pre-registered gift_cards row pending forever, which reads as money in with no credit (lh-money-escrow review of Q1750, 2026-10-15).** stripe-webhook acknowledges and does not apply such a checkout.session.completed or .expired, so the row create-gift-card-checkout pre-registered (payment_status pending) is never closed out, the shape checkoutSessionExpired.ts says must be reported. Only reachable if the checkout rule is bypassed (the checkout refuses a non-test recipient before Stripe). Measured 2026-10-15: 0 gift_cards rows with a cs_test_ session. Fix: on that refusal mark the pending row expired (zero rows is fine), or have the reconciliation skip cs_test_ rows. Done when an edge test of the refused delivery asserts the row is closed. finding: lh-money-escrow@f5d7fee1d#3
- [ ] **Q?test-gift-recipient-source LOW A test-to-test gift picked by name is checked against the login email at checkout but against profiles.email in the webhook, so the two could disagree (lh-money-escrow review of Q1750, 2026-10-15).** create-gift-card-checkout resolves a picked recipient's email with auth.admin.getUserById; testGiftIsTestToTest (_shared/stripeMode.ts) finds the recipient by profiles.email. If those ever differ, a test charge completes and the gift is acknowledged but not applied. Measured 2026-10-15: 0 profiles whose profiles.email differs from auth.users.email (all 57 test accounts match). Fix: carry the resolved recipient id in the session metadata and have testGiftIsTestToTest require that id to be a test account whose profiles.email equals recipient_email. Done when an edge test with a mismatched email pins the outcome. finding: lh-money-escrow@f5d7fee1d#2
- [ ] **Q?gift-live-credit-test-job LOW A LIVE gift card sent to a test account can be spent on a test-mode job, so the real buyer's money stays in the live balance while the job pays out in test mode (found building Q1750, 2026-10-15).** Lead decision for Q1750: a real buyer's gift checkout stays LIVE even when the recipient is a test account. Code read (not exercised): a wholly gift-funded job has no stored Checkout Session, so release-payout's jobMoneyMode decides by the job's parties, and a test poster with a test Helpr pays out on the TEST key. The live gift money then sits in the platform's live balance with no real person paid. Needs a decision: keep (it is the buyer's own choice and nothing real leaves), or have a gift carry its mode (gift_cards.stripe_session_id prefix) so a live gift funds only live jobs. Done when a test pins whichever rule is chosen.
- [ ] **Q?redrive-stored-mode LOW A held tip or won-chargeback re-pay decides its Stripe mode from today's test-account enrolment, not from where the money sits (lh-money-escrow review of Q1753, 2026-10-15).** For a job with no cs_ prefix (gift-funded), an account enrolled or un-enrolled between the charge and the re-drive sends transfers.create to the other key; Stripe refuses it as a non-transient error, so the row goes 'failed' with a critical "pay by hand" page. No money is lost; someone is paged wrongly. Measured 2026-10-15: 0 open tip_hold_redrives rows and 0 open held chargeback_clawbacks rows. Fix: store the event's livemode on the row when the webhook writes it (a nullable stripe_mode column), re-drive on that mode (still checked against the payees), and fall back to clientForJob only when it is null. Done when an edge test with a stored mode that differs from today's enrolment pins it. finding: lh-money-escrow@886256a04#1
